# OTR Bug Log

**Repo:** `ComfyUI-OldTimeRadio` @ `v2.0-alpha`
**Owner:** Jeffrey A. Brick

**Sprint marker:** Cast name<->gender<->voice coherence -- Phases 1+2 + S3 (2026-05-30) [SHIPPED on `v2.0-alpha` through `31ea332`; full OTR suite green except the 5 known pre-existing failures; pool mode byte-identical (C7)] -- Fixes the reported incoherence where male-coded names landed on female slots/voices and vice versa (`MALIK HIBBERT`->female, `PHYLLIS OKAFOR`->male). The voice always followed gender correctly; only the NAME was wrong. Root cause: `pick_first_last` (`config/cast_pools.py`) rolled from the flat gender-blind `FIRST_NAMES` pool while `precompute_ensemble_slots` (`nodes/_otr_casting.py:601-608`) bound the 40/40/20 gender plan to slots POSITIONALLY, never reconciling name vs gender. Fix = three commits executing `docs/2026-05-30-cast-system__go-forward-sprint-plan.md`: **S1** `acc091a` -- `FIRST_NAMES_BY_GENDER` (98 male / 24 female / 31 unisex; partitions the SAME names, `FIRST_NAMES` left byte-identical), `gender_of_first_name()`, `FIRST_NAMES_BY_GENRE`+`names_for_genre()` (`OTR_CAST_GENRE` default `auto`=full pool), import-time drift guard. **S0** `a0bd03b` -- frozen env contract `nodes/_otr_cast_env.py` (`OTR_NAME_MODE`/`OTR_CAST_GENRE`/`OTR_NAME_CROSS_GENDER_RATE`/`OTR_OTHER_NAME_POLICY`), golden pool baseline `tests/golden/cast_pool_baseline.json` (coherence-verified seed=1; known-incoherent seed=0 = NEMO-on-female), `tests/test_cast_invariants.py` (R1/R2/R4/R5/R6), `test_cast.sh`. **S2** `30c666d` -- `_repair_ensemble_names` swaps ONLY a mismatched first name for a same-gender one via a per-character ISOLATED rng `random.Random(f"{cast_seed}:{char_id}")`, so the main cast rng is NEVER perturbed: byte-identical for an already-coherent seed (R2), full coherence otherwise (R3); `cast_seed` threaded writer->lock_cast->precompute; standalone `cast_one_character` keeps `repair_names=False` (honours an explicitly-named character); `OTR_NAME_CROSS_GENDER_RATE` (default 0.0 strict) gates deliberate cross-gender names. PD1 audio-king: pool mode (default) is byte-identical -- the repair is a verified no-op at the coherent golden seed. PD3: no `INPUT_TYPES`/widget/socket change -> no workflow JSON re-wire (every knob is an env var). PD6: no new LLM call site (Phase 1 is pure-Python). Regression: full `tests/` walk -- only the 5 known pre-existing failures (test_b7_forbidden_sweep x2, test_llm_slot_sweep, test_bark_freeze_halt_bypass, test_workflow_validator_empty_path_fallback), 0 new; R1-R6+R3 green; BOM/AST/0-byte sweep clean. **Phase 2 + S3 ALSO SHIPPED this session:** S4 `_otr_castplanner.py` (immutable CastPlanSlot) + S7 `_otr_cast_validator.py` (Pass-1 referee: rejects out-of-contract keys / unknown-missing char_id / wrong count / dup names) + S5 voice x age (optional age_band on python_assign_voice_preset; pool passes None -> byte-identical; still ONE rng.choice/slot, R6) + S6 `_apply_llm_slot_fill` in lock_cast (OTR_NAME_MODE=llm_slot_fill: the deterministic S2-coherent cast is built FIRST as the guaranteed backstop, then ONE creative-slot LLM call -- reuses creative_fn, `# LLM slot: creative`, no new model_id widget (PD6) -- proposes name+texture per slot, validated by S7, a successful name still passes the S2 gender-repair as a strict-mode backstop; on ANY failure the deterministic names stand, no retry) + S8/R9 names final at lock_cast (frozen before dialogue) + S3 `_otr_writer_vram.py` (post-script LLM unload before TTS, never raises, gated by OTR_WRITER_UNLOAD_AFTER_SCRIPT, default on). Commits: S1 `acc091a` / S0 `a0bd03b` / S2 `30c666d` / docs `6479d27` / S4-S8 `7e04dc4` / S3 `31ea332`. Mode-matrix green: test_cast_invariants (pool, byte-identical) + test_cast_llm_naming (llm, 17 tests) + test_writer_llm_unload (4). PD3: no widget/INPUT_TYPES/socket -> no workflow-JSON re-wire. **OPERATOR FOLLOW-UPS (cannot be done headless):** (a) GPU smoke to confirm the S3 VRAM-envelope benefit (peak VRAM across the writer->TTS boundary); (b) a real-LLM episode with OTR_NAME_MODE=llm_slot_fill to validate Pass-1 naming end-to-end (headless tests use stubs); (c) Jeffrey's OTR_NAME_CROSS_GENDER_RATE lane decision (default strict 0.0 in place); (d) Bug Bible regression RAN against the OTR pack (operator-uploaded `bug_bible_regression.py` + `conftest.py`, run with `--pack-dir`) -- **23/23 applicable static checks PASS** (encoding/no-BOM, no 0-byte, AST-parse, registration/no-ghost-nodes, INPUT_TYPES, VRAM discipline [no module-scope model loads, unload->empty_cache], subprocess cleanup, workflow-JSON integrity, no `.get("completed",True)`), incl. every new cast file; 1 xfail (soft LLM length-guard note), 1 skip (OTR-local SD1.5 anchor_gen not on host). The ONLY failure is the survival-guide's OWN Three-File Contract (`test_entry_count_matches_readme` needs the guide's `BUG_BIBLE.yaml`/`README.md`, absent here) -- not an OTR/code issue. NOTE: the `comfyui-custom-node-survival-guide` dir is an inaccessible reparse point in this environment (cmd `dir` -> "syntax incorrect"), so the Three-File promotion (new YAML entry + README count + regression test in lockstep) is deferred to an operator session that can reach it. (e) `git tag v2.0` is Jeffrey's call (CLAUDE.md). Not a bug entry; no body section.

**New entry:** BUG-LOCAL-295 (2026-05-31) [LOGGED -- bounded fix proposed, operator review; NOT shipped per no-overhaul] -- The story-quality baseline (`docs/2026-05-31-otr-story-quality-baseline.md`) surfaced a cast-name leak into line BODY text on the REMOTE 350-word parity run (T4 "Muddy Canaries", `openrouter:slot-a` = `mistralai/mistral-nemo`): the ALL-CAPS canonical cast name landed where a verb/noun belongs -- `* ERIN SPENDER the monkeys' enclosure*` (a stage direction missing its verb) and `safe in the ERIN SPENDER` (a dangling noun). The LOCAL 350-word run (T3 "Doorway to Anomaly", SAME model both slots) was clean across all 16 speech lines, so this is a remote-Mistral-Nemo GENERATION artifact, **NOT a transport/wiring defect** -- every structured call returned valid JSON and the run completed end-to-end (the OpenRouter parity is proven; see the baseline doc's "Parity verdict -- CLOSED"). Why the existing sanitizer misses it: the BUG-LOCAL-279-follow-on leak filter in `nodes/_otr_line_composer.py` (~L1663-1692) only retries when the WHOLE cleaned line equals the speaker's own name or the literal `ANNOUNCER`, and DELIBERATELY does not filter the broader roster (to preserve legitimate cross-character one-word drama, e.g. `"Maeve."`). F3 is the uncaught case -- a roster cast name embedded MID-PHRASE / inside an asterisk `*...*` stage direction. PROPOSED bounded fix (operator review, gated on no-overhaul + the read-quality decision): in the compose retry loop, flag a draft where an ALL-CAPS MULTI-WORD roster name appears inside a `*...*` group or as a bare mid-sentence token and retry; scope strictly to multi-word ALL-CAPS so single-name drama is untouched; must pass Bug Bible + audio byte-identical before ship. Also seen, lower priority (F1 family): one mild speaker MISATTRIBUTION on T4 (a line addressing "Creed" tagged as Creed) -- largely a low-word-count artifact (T3 local 350w had perfect attribution; T1 remote 60w had a full collapse). **Tags:** line-composer, name-leak, remote-llm, dialogue-pass, quality-baseline. **Bible candidate:** TBD (pending fix verification). No body section.

**New entry:** BUG-LOCAL-294 (2026-05-31) [FIXED 2026-05-31] -- OpenRouter remote calls were **truncated**, not following-prompts-poorly. First ENABLED smoke (creative slot = `anthropic/claude-opus-4.8` remote, technical = local Mistral-Nemo, full video workflow) aborted at casting: `llm_write_description` returned `JSONDecodeError: no decodable top-level JSON object found: line 1 column 1 (char 0)` on all 3 attempts -> `CastValidationLLMError`. Looked like "the frontier model won't emit our JSON," but a direct OpenRouter capture proved otherwise: **the response was being truncated.** Root cause: `OpenRouterBackend.generate` clamped the remote `max_tokens` to the writer's per-call `max_new_tokens` (~150-200), a budget that only works on the LOCAL path because lm-format-enforcer forces a *compact bare* JSON object. A free-form remote model writes a fuller object (+ a ` ```json ` fence) and gets cut mid-object (`finish_reason=length`) -> no complete `{...}` -> the char-0 parse failure -> fail-closed. (`nodes/_otr_json.parse_first_json_object` already handles fences + leading prose; fences were NOT the issue.) Reproduced exactly via direct API: `max_tokens=50` -> `finish_reason=length` + truncated + char-0; `max_tokens=512` -> `finish_reason=stop` + clean parse. **Fix:** `nodes/_otr_openrouter_backend.py` -- new `DEFAULT_MIN_OUTPUT_TOKENS=1024` floor (env `OPENROUTER_MIN_OUTPUT_TOKENS`); `out_tokens = clamp(max_new_tokens, [floor, per-slot cap])`. `max_tokens` is a CEILING (the model stops at `finish_reason=stop` and bills only actual tokens), so flooring it costs nothing on short replies. Added a `finish_reason=length` warning log so future truncation is visible. +2 tests (`test_small_max_new_tokens_is_floored`, `test_floor_overridable_via_env`); updated `test_generate_happy_path` (128 -> floored 1024). Regression: full OTR `tests/` 3268 passed / 12 skipped / 0 failed. **Verified LIVE:** after relaunch, the enabled Opus run passed casting + the full outline (5 beats, all on Opus) -- the remote creative path works with the existing prompts; truncation was the blocker. PD1/PD3/PD6 N/A (backend-only; no node surface/widget/workflow change; default-off). **Secondary, non-blocking (BUG-263 family, separate):** Opus is verbose and overran the `character_description` 750-char schema cap; the `structured_call` repair ladder recovered (shortened on the typed-repair attempt) but burned extra calls -- a tuning item (raise the cap or prompt for brevity), not a wiring bug. **Design follow-up (operator round-robin in progress):** `docs/2026-05-31-openrouter-remote-creative-json__{problem,answer}-statement.md` -- "script-first, parse-second" (free-form generation + grammar-locked extraction) as the quality/robustness upgrade, now optional rather than required since truncation is fixed. **Tags:** openrouter, truncation, max-tokens, remote-llm, json, fail-closed. **Bible candidate:** yes -- "remote/API LLM needs an explicit output-token floor; the local grammar-era per-call budget truncates free-form remote output." No body section.

**New entry:** BUG-LOCAL-293 (2026-05-31) [FIXED 2026-05-31] -- The OpenRouter remote-LLM S0 baseline-lock (`docs/openrouter-remote-llm-go-forward-plan.md`) found the full `tests/` walk RED at HEAD `688263b` -- the 5 long-carried "known pre-existing failures" (named verbatim in the cast-coherence marker above). All 5 fixed so the OpenRouter build starts from a genuinely green baseline (Jeffrey-approved 2026-05-31; these are test/tooling/JSON-widget only, zero feature/audio code). **(1)** `test_workflow_validator_empty_path_fallback::test_empty_path_resolves_to_default` -- stale `>= 30` node-count floor; the canonical workflow is now **29** nodes after the 2026-05-30 LTX distilled-LoRA consolidation (`bfc761a`, nodes 60/61 -> one loader). Floor lowered to `>= 25` (intent = confirm the empty-path fallback resolved the real full workflow, not a stub) + the stale "34 nodes" comment de-staled. **(2)** `test_bark_freeze_halt_bypass::TestWorkflowJsonNode11::test_node_11_widgets_values_includes_bypass_default` -- canonical `otr_scifi_16gb_full.json` node 11 (`OTR_BatchBarkGenerator`) shipped `bypass_freeze_halt=true` (a 05/30 smoke-iteration leftover, cf. the BUG-276 investigation note below), which DISABLES the BUG-276 production-safe `needs_full_rerun` halt. Restored `widgets_values[2]` to `false` (the documented INPUT_TYPES default + BUG-276 out-of-box behaviour). **WORKFLOW JSON fix, not a test edit -- the test was correct.** **(3)** `test_llm_slot_sweep::test_every_llm_call_site_has_slot_tag` -- the technical-slot pass `generate_fn(...)` for SlotJobFields at `nodes/_otr_slot_drama_contract.py:384` was missing its `# LLM slot:` tag; added `# LLM slot: technical` (PD6 -- directly relevant since the OpenRouter build adds LLM call sites under the same C7 rule). **(4+5)** `test_b7_forbidden_sweep::{test_forbidden_sweep_runs_clean,test_forbidden_sweep_non_llm_marker_works}` -- `_run_sweep_subprocess` wrote the ~4 MB regenerated `s29-clean-slate-gate..worktree` diff INTO the OneDrive-synced `docs/` tree; OneDrive held the multi-MB file open mid-sync so the next truncating re-open raised `OSError(22) Invalid argument` at OPEN time (before any write; even `'wb'` failed; `unlink` -> `PermissionError: the process cannot access the file because it is being used by another process`). Hardened the tooling to write the temp diff/out files to the OS temp dir (NOT synced): `docs/_s28_forbidden_sweep.py` `DIFF_PATH`/`OUT_PATH` now honour `OTR_S28_DIFF_PATH`/`OTR_S28_OUT_PATH` env overrides (docs/ defaults unchanged for manual use); `tests/test_b7_forbidden_sweep.py::_run_sweep_subprocess` points them at `tempfile.gettempdir()`. Sweep with the freshly-regenerated diff = **runtime 0** (every `legacy fallback`/`shim`/`alias`/`back-compat` hit classifies as a forensic comment/string, not runtime code). Regression: full OTR `tests/` **3204 passed / 12 skipped / 0 failed** (was: these 5 failing, 0 new); Bug Bible **23 passed / 1 skipped / 2 xfailed / 0 failed**; `tools/audit_workflow_schema.py` zod-audit **OK (0 issues)**. KNOWN-LIMITATION (NOT this change): `scripts/_audit_workflow_json.py` still reports `AUDIT FAILED: unregistered_types` for 6 nodes -- environmental, identical pre/post: 4 OTR nodes can't import outside the ComfyUI process (`No module named 'folder_paths'`) + 2 ComfyUI built-ins (`LoraLoaderModelOnly`, `LowVRAMCheckpointLoader`) are never in OTR's registry; node 11 loaded fine and a boolean widget flip can't change type registration. The authoritative pytest workflow-wiring suite (`test_workflow_json_*`, `test_default_workflow_validator`, `test_workflow_canonical_baseline`) is green. PD1 audio-king: node-11 bypass restored to the SAFE default (production halt re-enabled) -- strengthens, never weakens, C1; the audio byte-identical RUNTIME gate is operator-driven (`OTR_REGRESSION_RUNTIME`) and unaffected by test/tooling/widget-value edits. PD3: workflow JSON edited (node 11 widget VALUE only -- no node surface/socket/INPUT_TYPES change), zod-schema audit green -> no re-wire needed. **Tags:** baseline-lock, json-wiring, slot-tag, onedrive, ci-tooling, widget-drift. **Bible candidate:** yes -- the OneDrive-synced-temp-file `OSError(22)` pattern is a reusable Windows/OneDrive CI gotcha (write multi-MB CI temp files to the OS temp dir, never into a synced repo tree). No body section.

**Investigation (2026-05-29) -- BUG-LOCAL-276 RECURRENCE confirmed + deeper root-cause localized (no code changed; investigation only):** Phase-2 lean-down live-render validation, HEAD `41c23d6`, queued headless via `POST /prompt` (prompt_id `140c9d67-427b-49f9-86d5-c0185ccf9646`, episode `pending_20260529_152607` "Saturn's Pulse"). Writer ran clean end-to-end (news -> style -> outline -> cast -> dialogue; Mistral-Nemo NF4 loaded from `C:\ComfyUI-Models\huggingface`). The story critic flagged the dialogue (arc=uneven, 3 flat lines), the Sprint-5C targeted reroll exhausted its 2 cycles, and the freeze cascade returned `freeze_verdict=needs_full_rerun` (skipped Phases 7-10). Node 11 `bypass_freeze_halt=ON` (the workflow's smoke-iteration default) so BUG-276's `needs_full_rerun` halt was BYPASSED and Bark rendered the flagged ledger -- reproducing the ORIGINAL BUG-276 crash at `batch_bark_generator.py:520` (Gate 3): `BatchBarkGenerator: cast.voice_preset missing or non-v2/* for character 'ANNOUNCER' (line_id=b002, char_id='c01', got 'bm_fable')`. **Takeaway: BUG-276's hotfix only protects the `bypass=OFF` path; with `bypass=ON` the deeper (parked) defect is still live.** Ledger evidence (`pending_20260529_152607_ledger.json`): cast c01=ANNOUNCER(`bm_fable`/Kokoro), c02=JAMES MARTIN(`v2/en_speaker_5`), c03=ANDREW HALLOWAY(`v2/en_speaker_9`); SIX `speaker_role='character'` lines (b002/b004/b007/b010/b014/b016) carry `char_id='c01'` (the announcer), the main conversational pair landed as c01<->c03 with c02 used only on b009/b011. ROOT-CAUSE LOCALIZATION (this is the deeper fix BUG-276 parked): the announcer leaks into the CHARACTER speaker pool only on the `needs_full_rerun` reroll path. The MAIN writer path correctly EXCLUDES the announcer -- `OTR_LedgerScriptWriter.py:2309-2312` builds `char_id_by_name` with `if row["name"] != "ANNOUNCER"`, so `character_cast` + every char_id stamp (L2501 `init_lines_from_outline`, L3219/L3341) can only resolve to c02/c03, never c01. The migrated Stage-3 reroll path does NOT replicate that exclusion: `_otr_legacy_to_stage1_adapter.py:226-294` builds `stage1_cast` / `valid_cast_names` from ALL cast rows with no announcer filter (placeholder fallback `speaker=stage1_cast[0].name` = the announcer at L385), and `_otr_reroll.py:168` `name_by_id` maps char_id->name from all rows -- so the reroll treats c01/ANNOUNCER as a regular character (live log: `[OTR_LineComposer] attempt 1/2 for ANNOUNCER`). Lean-down-adjacent: that adapter was migrated in LEAN_DOWN step 5. The 6-c01-lines vs 5-rerolled-lines count suggests the synthetic-led rebuild re-derives char_ids for the whole line set from the full (announcer-included) cast, not only the rerolled beats; the exact introduction point (original outline speaker->char_id map vs reroll synthetic-led rebuild) is still to be pinned. PROPOSED FIX (parked, needs operator review): mirror the L2312 `!= "ANNOUNCER"` exclusion in the reroll/Stage-3 cast pool + char_id remap, AND add a Gate-1/Gate-2 pre-Bark enforcement that rejects any `speaker_role='character'` line whose resolved char_id is an announcer/non-`v2/*` voice -- so the contract violation surfaces at the writer, not at render. Interlocks with BUG-LOCAL-241 (freeze_verdict advisory-vs-blocking) and BUG-LOCAL-279 (small-budget length undershoot -> critic flag -> the `needs_full_rerun` trigger). Verify a future fix by forcing an episode into `needs_full_rerun` and asserting no `speaker_role='character'` line resolves to an announcer char_id. **Bible candidate: yes** (cast-routing contract; pairs with the BUG-276 family). Phase-2 launch note for the toolkit: headless OTR must be launched with `HF_HOME=C:\ComfyUI-Models\huggingface` set BEFORE `main.py` (the prestartup only auto-sets it when unset, and would otherwise point at the empty `Documents\ComfyUI\models\huggingface`); use a .bat (`set` on its own line) -- an inline `set VAR=val && ...` appends a trailing space that breaks the transformers loader's `os.path.join(HF_HOME,'hub')`. **CORRECTION (same-session deeper trace -- supersedes the reroll/adapter attribution above):** the reroll is NOT the speaker-corruption site. `_otr_reroll.py` mutates line TEXT in place only (`update_line_text`; rows are never re-stamped -- see its L271-391 comments), and `production_ledger.init_lines_from_outline` (L748-753) maps a character beat's speaker via `char_id_by_name.get(speaker, "")` -> a valid character name yields c02/c03 and an off-map/announcer name yields "" (NEVER c01). So c01 is written onto the b002/b004/b007/b010/b014/b016 character lines by a re-stamp pass that runs BETWEEN init and the final ledger -- the cast-contract auditor/repair (`audit_cast_contract:pre` ran this episode; cf. BUG-LOCAL-271, whose `wrong_char_id` repair already mishandles expected=`c01`/`c02`). Structural smell + leading candidate: the cast CONTRACT assigns character_ids by ALPHABETICAL canonical-name order (`_otr_cast_contract.py:260-263`, "c01, c02, ... in that sort order"), a DIFFERENT scheme from the writer roster's announcer-first c01 (announcer pre-baked at c01 in `_otr_casting.py:948-951`). A pass that re-stamps line char_ids in the contract's alphabetical space while the ledger roster stays announcer-first is the most likely mechanism for a character line resolving to the announcer's voice. Exact re-stamp write site still to be pinned (cast-contract auditor in the freeze cascade). The reroll/adapter announcer-inclusion (`_otr_legacy_to_stage1_adapter.py:226-294`, `_otr_reroll.py:168`) is a real latent issue but is NOT the c01 source. Operator design steer (Jeffrey 2026-05-29): lock speaker/char_id at composition; no later pass (auditor/reroll) should remap a character line onto the announcer, and announcer lines should be left unmanaged by the reroll. **FIX LANDED 2026-05-29 (Gate-2 guard, pending live soak):** `nodes/_otr_ledger_reviewer.py::apply_deterministic_cast_repairs` -- the `wrong_char_id` branch now REFUSES to write a `cid` whose cast row is the announcer (name=="ANNOUNCER", id-scheme independent) or carries a present-but-non-`v2/*` voice onto a `speaker_role=='character'` line; the row is left for the Script Doctor (not counted as repaired) so the locked character speaker stays constant. New `char_id->cast row` map added at the repair setup. An EMPTY `voice_preset` does NOT trigger the refusal (unit fixtures carry none). +2 regression tests `tests/test_phase3_ledger_reviewer.py::TestBugLocal276RefuseAnnouncerOnCharacterLine` (refuse announcer-onto-character; positive-control character->character still repairs). Gates green 2026-05-29: targeted cast/reviewer suites 104 passed; Bug Bible regression 23 passed / 1 skipped / 2 xfailed / 0 failed; `test_core.py` 59 passed; `test_audio_byte_identical.py` 9 passed / 1 skipped (PD1 byte-identity held). No node `INPUT_TYPES`/widget/socket change -> no workflow JSON re-wire (PD3 N/A); no new LLM call site (PD6 N/A). STILL OPEN (follow-ups, not fixed by this guard): (a) WHY the `audit_cast_contract:pre` LLM suggests the announcer's char_id for a character beat in the first place; (b) the dual char_id scheme -- writer roster announcer-first c01 (`_otr_casting.py:948`) vs cast-contract alphabetical c01 (`_otr_cast_contract.py:260`) -- should be unified; (c) `bypass_freeze_halt=ON` still renders a flagged ledger (BUG-276 halt only fires when OFF); (d) Bible promotion (Three-File Contract) deferred until live-soak verified. Fix not yet active in the running ComfyUI (module reload needs a restart); live confirm requires a re-queue that hits `needs_full_rerun`.

**New entry:** BUG-LOCAL-280 (2026-05-29) [FIXED 2026-05-29] -- Headless smoke harness `scripts/queue_smoke.py` could never queue. It called `patch_widget_by_name(wf, 1, "act_count", 1, schemas)` with integer `1`, but `act_count` on `OTR_LedgerScriptWriter` is a COMBO of STRINGS `['auto','1','2','3','4','5','6','7']`; `otr_api._validate_widget_value` correctly rejected the int (`got 1 which is not in the choice list`). The `target_words=30` + `num_characters=2` patches succeeded first, so it read as "smoke just won't run." Fix: `1` -> `"1"` (one char, `scripts/queue_smoke.py:58`). Surfaced live 2026-05-29 launching the 30-word headless API smoke on the post-lean-down build (HEAD `b5c1e93`). **Tags:** smoke-harness, widget-combo. Not Bible-promoted (dev-harness one-liner; fix not yet committed). No body section.

**New entry:** BUG-LOCAL-281 (2026-05-29) [RECLASSIFIED 2026-05-29 -- CONVERTER-SIDE; workflow JSON is CORRECT, do NOT edit it] -- Canonical workflow `workflows/otr_scifi_16gb_full.json` node 14 (`OTR_MusicGenTheme`) carries `widgets_values` of length 5, but the live `/object_info` schema for the current node exposes only 4 serialized widget slots. `scripts/otr_api.workflow_to_api_prompt` refuses the UI->API conversion: `widgets_values length mismatch on node 14 (OTR_MusicGenTheme): len(wv)=5 vs len(serialized_slots)=4`. **Pre-existing widget-drift, NOT from the 2026-05-29 dead-code lean-down** -- node 14's module is untouched and it reproduces on a clean tree at `b5c1e93`. Impact: `queue_smoke.py` (which does live UI->API conversion) is blocked; the prebuilt-API path is NOT affected -- POSTing a patched `_otr_full_api.json` via `/prompt` was ACCEPTED with `node_errors={}`, so the api-json node 14 maps cleanly and the smoke ran (prompt_id `babe3b74`). **CORRECTION 2026-05-29 (regression gate caught it):** the workflow JSON is CORRECT and must NOT be edited. The 5-value vector MATCHES `MusicGenTheme.INPUT_TYPES()` (which counts `script_json` even though it is `forceInput`) and is pinned green by `tests/test_workflow_audio_widget_vectors.py` (7 tests). An agent attempt to drop node 14's leading `script_json` slot from 5->4 broke 3 of those pins (`test_musicgen_widget_vector_length_matches_input_types`, `test_musicgen_allow_silence_fallback_pinned_false`, `test_no_stale_dict_residue_in_widget_vector[OTR_MusicGenTheme]`) and was REVERTED via `git checkout` (tests back to 7 passed). The real defect is in `scripts/otr_api.py::workflow_to_api_prompt` (its `serialized_slots` computation), which wrongly EXCLUDES the forceInput `script_json` slot (computes 4) then raises against the correct 5-value vector. **Real fix (DEFERRED, dev-harness only):** make `workflow_to_api_prompt` count the forceInput-converted widget slot for length-matching but source its value from the wired link, not the widget value. LOW priority -- the prebuilt-API POST path (`_otr_full_api.json` -> `/prompt`) is unaffected and the smoke runs through it. Sibling of BUG-LOCAL-253 (node-14 widget-vector drift). **Tags:** converter, widget-drift, forceInput, musicgen. **Bible candidate:** yes (otr_api forceInput slot-count contract).

Existing TestTier2LengthEnforcement tests still green (target=10 band relaxed from [5..17] to [3..17], but assertions only check below/above-floor behavior, not exact band values). **Prior pointer:** the original BUG-LOCAL-279 [LOGGED] header line below is preserved verbatim for the investigation-log audit trail; the FIXED-status update lives in the "Last entry" line above, and the body entry `### BUG-LOCAL-279` carries the merged fix narrative.
**LOGGED-investigation snapshot (superseded by Last entry above):** BUG-LOCAL-279 (2026-05-26) [originally LOGGED 2026-05-26 16:59] -- The LineComposer consistently undershoots the lower drift band on small-budget smokes (60-110 words). Live evidence across the Sprint 10A operator soak (4 runs 2026-05-26 13:55..16:18): every character line shipped 2-8 words despite `target=20 words` and band `[10..34]`. The 2-attempt retry ladder never lands inside the band -- the `[OTR_LineComposer] shipping drifty line on final attempt 2` warning fires 100% of the time, the line ships sub-floor, the legacy story critic flags it as "flat", the Sprint 5C reroll loop spends both its cycles on the same line and exhausts. Final `freeze_verdict=needs_full_rerun` is then the EXPECTED outcome of the legacy critic loop -- there is nothing wrong with the cascade or BUG-LOCAL-276's halt; the BUG sits two layers earlier in the LineComposer's target/band math. Two compounding factors: (1) **Budget math** -- the writer splits 60 words / 3 character beats = 20 words/beat, but Mistral-Nemo on the creative slot at temp=0.85 prefers shorter dialogue (~5-15 words/line is its natural distribution). (2) **Band floor too strict for small targets** -- `_word_bands(20)` returns `min_words=max(3, int(20*0.5))=10`, which is exactly the 50% floor the live model misses. Three fix shapes for operator decision: (A) **lower the min floor on small targets** (`min_words = max(3, int(target * 0.4))` for target <= 25), preserves the band for 30-50 word targets where the model converges fine; (B) **change beat allocation** -- 60 words / 2 char beats × 1 line × 30 words each, fewer-but-longer-lines bias model toward landing inside the band; (C) **prompt-level nudge** in `_otr_line_composer._build_user_prompt` -- "produce at least N words" instruction tied to `min_words`. Decision pending. Non-blocking on Sprint 10A code-completeness (the legacy critic loop is independent of Sprint 10A; the bypass_freeze_halt widget is the smoke-iteration escape hatch). See body `### BUG-LOCAL-279`.
**Prior entry:** BUG-LOCAL-278 (2026-05-26) [FIXED 2026-05-26 -- pending soak verification] -- Live soak run `signal_lost_..._154105` (Sprint 10A step-7 wiring loaded, build `c86fda8` post-Desktop-restart) surfaced a load-bearing persistence gap. The freeze cascade modifies `meta` in place at every exit (`freeze_verdict`, `freeze_disposition`, `story_critic_report`, `stage7_shadow_critic`, `freeze_phase_telemetry`) but the cascade itself does NOT save the ledger to disk on the terminal-skip / Phase-10-reject / successful-freeze exits. In-memory readers (`BatchBarkGenerator`, `MusicGenTheme`, socket-fed downstream nodes) SEE the cascade's stamps from the live `led.data` reference -- BUG-LOCAL-276's halt fires correctly off `meta.freeze_verdict='needs_full_rerun'`. But disk readers DON'T -- `KokoroAnnouncer.generate` calls `_OTRL.load_ledger_safe(ledger_path)` then `save_ledger_safe(ledger_path, led_disk)`, reading the PRE-cascade ledger from disk, stamping its own line_id fields, and writing back. The cascade's modifications are silently dropped. Symptom: Step 8 operator gate criteria (`meta.stage1_shadow_attempts`, `meta.stage7_shadow_critic`, etc.) MISSING from the persisted ledger.json after the run; the operator cannot verify the gate from disk; forensic tools see a stale .json. Pre-existing architectural gap; predates Sprint 10A by months (the BUG-LOCAL-274 render_plan-persistence fix added the same save pattern at ONE exit; the other three exits stayed unfixed). Fix shipped: new `_persist_cascade_meta(led)` helper at top of `nodes/_otr_freeze_cascade.py`; called at every cascade exit (terminal-skip from reviewer-terminal verdicts, reroll-exhaustion terminal-skip, Phase 10 rejection, successful freeze). PD1: defensive getattr -- handles test SimpleNamespace fixtures without `save`; production `Ledger.save()` never raises; persist failures log warning + continue. +5 regression tests in `tests/test_freeze_cascade_meta_persistence.py::TestBug278CascadeMetaPersistedAtEveryExit` -- spy on `_persist_cascade_meta` to assert wiring fires at every exit, plus broken-save + SimpleNamespace fixtures pin the PD1 paths. Regression: full OTR suite 3251 passed / 20 skipped / 0 failed (was 3246 + 5 new tests); Bug Bible 23 passed / 1 skipped / 2 xfailed / 0 failed; LLM-slot sweep N/A (no LLM call site added). PD invariants: PD1 N/A (forensic only, never raises); PD3 N/A (no node surface change); PD6 N/A (no LLM call). See body `### BUG-LOCAL-278`.

**Prior entry:** BUG-LOCAL-275 (2026-05-25) [FIXED 2026-05-25] -- Sprint 7C lane. Added a dedicated `payload_null_repair` typed-prompt factory in `nodes/_otr_repair_prompts.py`. The `make_dispatching_repair_factory` dispatcher now detects pydantic ValidationErrors whose repr contains BOTH `payload` and `input_value=None` and routes them to the new factory before falling through to the generic `schema_field_repair` (Sprint 2C kept that path intact for every other ValidationError). The new directive is explicit: every `payload` MUST be a non-null replacement string; if the model has no replacement text, it must OMIT the entire edit row rather than emit `payload: null` / `payload: ""`. Schema stays strict (`ReviewerEdit.payload: str`) so a null still fails fast at the schema gate; the recovery is typed, not the schema widening (option (a) would have masked intentional doctor mistakes). Regression: `tests/test_repair_prompts.py` 18 -> 21 (+3 -- new builder test + 2 dispatcher routing tests including a `payload`-on-an-unrelated-schema specificity guard); full OTR suite 2856 passed / 21 skipped / 0 failed (baseline 2853 + 3 new); Bug Bible 16 passed / 7 skipped / 3 xfailed / 0 failed; LLM-slot sweep 23/23 tagged. No node `INPUT_TYPES` / widget / output socket touched -- no workflow JSON re-wire (Prime Directive 3 N/A). No new LLM call site -- the typed factory only reshapes the prompt of the existing Attempt 3 repair call (Prime Directive 6 N/A).

**Prior entry:** BUG-LOCAL-273 (2026-05-25) [FIXED 2026-05-25] -- See body `### BUG-LOCAL-273`. Root cause: production_ledger.Ledger.save() rebinds self.data to a new merged dict on every save (p_ledger.py:1012, BUG-LOCAL-108 merge-with-disk pattern). The Phase 1+2 reviewer at cascade L622 calls save() internally, detaching the cascade's L570/L576 `ledger_data`/`meta` locals from the live ledger. The Sprint 5B critic stamp at L683 landed on the orphaned dict; the reroll (L505) and render_plan (L162) re-read `led.data` and got the live dict with no critic report -- `StoryCriticReport.model_validate({})` succeeded with defaults (arc_verdict="strong", empty reroll_targets/flat_lines) because every field has a default, so no warning fired. Fix: refresh `ledger_data = led.data` + `meta = ledger_data.setdefault("meta", {})` before both the Sprint 5B critic stamp and the Sprint 6 render_plan stamp. The dict is now the live one at every stamp site. Original live-run evidence: cascade log `[LFC] Sprint 5B story critic: arc_verdict=uneven, 4 reroll target(s), 4 flat line(s) ...` followed by `[OTR_Reroll] critic named no reroll targets -- nothing to do` and `[LFC] Sprint 6 render plan: ... arc_verdict=strong ... 0 flat line(s) excluded` -- all three downstream defaults instead of the critic's real output. Regression coverage as above. `[LFC] Sprint 5B story critic: arc_verdict=uneven, 4 reroll target(s), 4 flat line(s), 0 continuity issue(s), 13 line(s) in render priority`. Eight lines later at L710 the reroll module re-read `meta.story_critic_report` via `_coerce_report` and logged `[OTR_Reroll] critic named no reroll targets -- nothing to do` -- 0 targets. The Sprint 6 render plan that ran next at L740 saw `arc_verdict=strong` (the `or "strong"` default in `_otr_render_plan.py:165`) + `0 flat line(s) excluded`. WRITE key (`_otr_freeze_cascade.py:683`) and READ keys (`_otr_reroll.py:505`, `_otr_render_plan.py:162`) ALL agree on `meta["story_critic_report"]` -- key naming is NOT the root cause. No `[OTR_Reroll] meta.story_critic_report did not validate` warning fired, ruling out a noisy pydantic re-validate failure. Two candidates remain: (A) silent lossy pydantic round-trip via `StoryCriticReport(...).model_dump()` -> `model_validate(dict)` -- a `model_config` setting / alias / `extra='forbid'` could be dropping list entries; (B) something replaces or empties the dict between L683 stamp and L505 read. Investigation is the first 7A work item: a sanity log between L683 and L710 will localize the wipe before the fix touches code. Non-blocking on PD1 (mp4 landed) but the entire reroll loop + arc-verdict gate is dark until fixed. See body `### BUG-LOCAL-273`.
**Prior entry:** BUG-LOCAL-272 (2026-05-25) [FIXED 2026-05-25] -- Found during the Sprint 4 VRAM-hardening code-side verify. `load_llm` (`nodes/_otr_model_loader.py`) resolves an attention-implementation selector (`attn_impl`: Flash Attention 2 when a `flash-attn` wheel is importable, else SDPA) and logs it -- but `common_kwargs` hardcoded `attn_implementation="sdpa"`, so the selector was dead code. Benign on the current Blackwell sm_120 / Windows / torch 2.10 stack (no FA2 wheel exists -> SDPA is correct either way), but if an FA2 wheel ever appeared the load log would announce `flash_attention_2` while the model still loaded SDPA. Fixed: `common_kwargs` now consumes `attn_impl` as the single source of truth + an explicit resolved-value log line fires on every load (completes the Sprint 4 "attention selector logged on every load" requirement). No runtime change on the current stack. Loader + tier suites green; full OTR suite 2787 passed. See body `### BUG-LOCAL-272`.

**Prior entry:** BUG-LOCAL-268 (2026-05-24) [FIXED 2026-05-24] -- `run_story_brief_reflection`'s schema-validation repair arm is dead code: `_repair_pass(failed_output=json_str, ...)` (`_otr_story_brief.py:648`) references `json_str`, a name never bound in scope (the raw LLM output is `raw`, bound at L604). **Prior entry:** BUG-LOCAL-267 (2026-05-24) [FIXED 2026-05-24] -- Character dialogue lines carried the speaker name as literal spoken text (ozempics_glitch ledger: b002 `text`/`text_for_tts` = `"HAYES VANCE: I'm worried..."`, b004 = `"HAYES VANCE not right."`) -- Bark voiced the character's name aloud. Cause: the composer strips a leading `"SPEAKER:"` prefix via `strip_line_formatting` on every composed line, but the Phase 3 ScriptDoctor reviewer runs AFTER composition and `apply_doctor_edits` wrote a `rewrite` edit's payload verbatim into `line["text"]` with no strip (`strip_line_formatting` is imported only in `OTR_LedgerScriptWriter.py` + `_otr_line_composer.py`, not the reviewer). **Prior entry:** BUG-LOCAL-266 (2026-05-24) [FIXED 2026-05-24] -- The BUG-265 verification run (episode `pending_20260524_171500`) crashed at the newly-shipped `OTR_HuMoTierLoader` (node 72) after 384 s: `RuntimeError: OTR_HuMoTierLoader: node 'AudioEncoderLoader' returned <comfy_api.latest._io.NodeOutput object>; expected a non-empty tuple.` ComfyUI 0.22.2 migrated the core `AudioEncoderLoader` node to its V3 node API, whose `FUNCTION` returns a `NodeOutput` wrapper (results on `.args`, exposed via `.result`) instead of a bare tuple; `OTR_HuMoTierLoader._invoke` validated `isinstance(tuple|list)` and raised. **Prior entry:** BUG-LOCAL-263 (2026-05-24) [FIXED 2026-05-24] -- The BUG-261/262 operator verification run, episode `pending_20260524_132257`, `google/gemma-2-2b-it` in BOTH model slots at `creativity='maximum chaos'`, aborted in cast lock: gemma-2-2b-it overruns strict Pydantic caps. `CastingResponse.character_description` exceeded its 200-char `max_length` on all 3 attempts for `HAYES VANCE` (`CastValidationLLMError`, fatal, run dead at 49.61 s); separately `NewsBriefs.key_terms` returned 10 items vs the 6-item cap (`news_interpreter` failed all attempts, fell back to raw `news_seed` -- non-fatal). NOT a parse failure -- BUG-261's consolidated `_otr_json` extractor held (zero `Extra data`); the failure is one stage downstream at Pydantic validation. The SAME run VERIFIED BUG-262 -- the style picker cleared cleanly with zero `TemplateError`. Fixed 2026-05-24 (Jeffrey's call -- "don't be pushy about length limits"): the real defect was a prompt/schema contract gap -- the casting prompt only ever said `"<short>"`, never a number, while the schema hard-enforced `max_length=200`. Closed in `_otr_casting.py` -- `character_description` `max_length` 200 -> 750 + prompt placeholder `<short>` -> `<vivid, 1-2 sentences>`. Regression green (test_otr_casting 57 passed, Bug Bible 23 passed). **Prior entry:** BUG-LOCAL-262 (2026-05-24) [FIXED 2026-05-24 -- operator-verified] -- An episode run aborted in the style picker with `StyleGenerationFailedError: chooser LLM call failed: TemplateError: System role not supported` when the technical-slot model was `google/gemma-2-2b-it`. Cause: Gemma-2 has no system role by design -- its Jinja chat template contains a literal `raise_exception("System role not supported")` -- and the writer generate path passed a message list containing a `{"role":"system",...}` entry straight into `tokenizer.apply_chat_template`; `gemma-4-E4B-it` + Mistral-Nemo accept a system role so the bug was latent until a Gemma-2-family model hit the technical slot. Fix: system-role normalization at the message-encoding boundary -- new `tokenizer_supports_system_role` probe + `normalize_messages_for_tokenizer` helper in `nodes/_otr_loader_backends.py` fold system content into the first user turn for any system-role-incompatible tokenizer (the standard Gemma pattern); the probe is cached once per model residency; every live `apply_chat_template` call site (writer `generate_fn`, loader `make_generate_fn`/`make_polish_generate_fn`, `visual/llm_polish.py`) plus `encode_messages_for_row`'s `transformers_default` branch route through it. A `google/gemma-2-2b-it` catalog row + license-audit file were added (`gated_terms`/`research_lane` -- Gemma-2 ships under the restricted Gemma Terms of Use, not Apache 2.0). Internal generate-path plumbing only -- no node/widget/socket/workflow-JSON change. New `tests/test_system_role_fold.py` 11 passed; dispatch + catalog suites green; audio byte-identical; Bug Bible 23 passed. See body `### BUG-LOCAL-262`.
**Prior entry:** BUG-LOCAL-260 (2026-05-23) [FIX LANDED 2026-05-23] -- The LEMMY easter-egg cameo (a rare ~11% character) appeared in the locked cast of 100% of runs. NOT an RNG bug -- `roll_lemmy` is statistically sound (10.76% LEMMY-positive over 20,000 seeds). Cause: a 2026-05-10 change routed the roll through the cast contract's seeded `random.Random` for C7; the writer's `seed` widget ships a FIXED value (`otr_scifi_16gb_full.json` node 1: `seed=42`, control `fixed`), and `random.Random(42)` is one of the ~11% LEMMY-positive seeds, so every fixed-seed run reproduced the hit. A fixed seed can only yield 0% or 100%, never ~11%. Fix: decoupled the roll -- `cast_pools.roll_lemmy()` now always uses OS entropy (`SystemRandom`), never the seed; cast names / announcer / style stay seed-deterministic. Also exposed a `lemmy_cameo` combo widget on `OTR_LedgerScriptWriter` (roll / always / never -> `force_lemmy`) so the operator keeps control. Full `tests/` walk 2520 passed / 17 skipped / 0 failed; Bug Bible 23 passed. See body `### BUG-LOCAL-260`.

**Prior (FLUX investigation) entry:** BUG-LOCAL-244 (2026-05-19 11:10) -- **FLUX fast-path mechanism UNIDENTIFIED.** Split off from BUG-LOCAL-231 per Jeffrey 11:10 pushback #5: "do not close 231 until the fast-path is either reproduced+explained or formally split." Two outliers observed against the ~180 s/it slow-regime baseline established by batteries v1 + v2: (1) **42.38 s/it** today 2026-05-19 ~08:55 (battery v1 iter 1) with precheck + 1 sampler-poll tick captured at start (precheck logged `cudnn.benchmark=False`, poll tick 1 logged 15954 MB lhm + 618 MB D3D Shared + 2977 MHz clocks); (2) **1.22 s/it** yesterday 2026-05-18 ~23:30 with NO sampler-time telemetry (predates commit 3691317). Speed-up ratios vs ~180 s/it slow cluster: 4.3x (42.38) and 150x (1.22). The 4.3x and 150x differ by 35x -- likely DIFFERENT mechanisms, not the same warm-cache cause. **Verification gate:** reproduce ≥1 fast run with the full battery v3 telemetry (per-step timestamps + precheck + poller across ≥5 steps) AND identify which signal differs between fast and slow runs, OR mark UNREPRODUCIBLE if no fast run captured in next N batteries. Hypothesis seed: cudnn / cublas kernel cache hit from an immediately-prior identical inference (process-local for 42.38, possibly cross-process / persistent for 1.22) -- but `cudnn.benchmark=False` rules out the autotuner; the 4.3x speed-up may be from a different cache layer (workspace allocator? cudnn forward conv plan? fp8 dequant kernel SASS cache?). **NOT a fix-it bug** -- a phenomenon-investigation bug. Pending: battery v3 with per-step timing + config fingerprint, then targeted attempt to reproduce.
**Prior 1 entry:** BUG-LOCAL-243 (2026-05-19) -- Live dialogue-line visibility regression in LFC writer. The legacy `OTR_LLMScriptWriter` streamed each composed dialogue line to ComfyUI console as the LLM produced it -- operator could watch the script unfold in real time, catch bad output early (vocative drift, hallucinated names, tone misses), and tell whether the LLM was making progress vs hung. The current `OTR_LedgerScriptWriter` writes silently to the ledger file and only emits `[Ledger] saved pending_..._ledger.json (N lines, M words)` at the end of the entire writer phase. No mid-composition visibility. Confirmed by Jeffrey 2026-05-19: would have caught BUG-LOCAL-233 vocative drift IMMEDIATELY ("Hold tight there, GULLIVER REEVES" would have streamed to console; operator could kill the run) instead of finding it in the saved ledger after the fact. **Fix shape:** add `log.info("[LineComposer] line_id=%s speaker=%s text=%s", line_id, speaker, text)` (or equivalent INFO-level emission) after each successful `compose_line` return in `nodes/_otr_line_composer.py`. Cheap, additive, no behavior change. Pending until pipeline closes end-to-end per pipeline-closes-first rule.
**Prior 2 entry:** BUG-LOCAL-242 (2026-05-19) -- Word/char count audit too permissive on text-vs-count drift. Recent ledgers show b007 word=18 / actual text words=19 (the inserted GULLIVER REEVES vocative); b007 char=112 / actual=121; b015 word=24 / actual=23. The §6.G word_counts audit only logs WARNING on drift, never blocks. **Decision needed:** tighten audit OR formally relax it. Hygiene, low priority. Pending design.
**Prior 3 entry:** BUG-LOCAL-241 (2026-05-19) -- `freeze_verdict='needs_full_rerun'` ignored by downstream. Cascade explicitly signals the run shouldn't proceed but the pipeline continues to audio + FLUX phases anyway. **Design clarification needed:** is the verdict advisory or supposed to block? If supposed to block, current ignore-and-continue is a bug. If advisory, the verdict label is misleading. Pending design.
**Prior 4 entry:** BUG-LOCAL-240 (2026-05-19) -- Freeze cascade style-slug validator out of sync with style picker's intended LLM-invent-slug behavior. Per Jeffrey 2026-05-19, the "let the story decide" sentinel IS designed to invent new slugs based on the story content -- Pass 1 inventor producing slugs like `geosynchronous_orbital_rivalry`, `particle_song_revealed`, `psyche_craft_arrival`, `ten_year_secret_hunt`, `clinical_trial_cliffhanger` is by-design and is the feature Jeffrey wants. The bug is on the validator side: the freeze cascade checks strict `slug in KNOWN_STYLE_SLUGS` membership and rejects anything outside the 10-preset seed palette, treating LLM-invented slugs as "writer drift". The validator was never updated to accept the picker's intended output. Recurring hygiene noise; the cascade flags this as a critical error pre + post every run but does not block the pipeline (related: BUG-LOCAL-241 advisory-vs-blocking question). **[FIXED 2026-05-21]** `_otr_ledger_freeze.py::_check_meta_invariants` -- the `slug in KNOWN_STYLE_SLUGS` membership check is replaced by `_is_well_formed_style_slug` (regex `^[a-z]+(_[a-z]+)*$` + 64-char cap). Invented snake_case slugs from the picker now pass; only malformed slugs (uppercase, spaces, digits, runaway length) are flagged. **Cause:** the validator dated to S25/MG-6 (BUG-216) when `musicgen_theme` looked up a cue palette keyed by the slug; `musicgen_theme` stopped consuming the slug as a palette key at Path F (2026-05-18) and composes prompts from the meta brief directly, so an unknown-but-well-formed slug halts nothing downstream -- the membership check was guarding a dead consumer. The now-unused `KNOWN_STYLE_SLUGS` import was pruned from `_otr_ledger_freeze.py`. `tests/test_style_palette_drift.py` updated in lockstep (`rejects_unknown_slug` -> `rejects_malformed_slug`; new `accepts_invented_snake_case_slug`). **Verify:** full `tests/` walk 2441 passed / 22 skipped / 0 failed (2026-05-21). **Bible candidate:** no (validator-staleness hygiene fix).

**Prior 8 entry:** BUG-LOCAL-236 (2026-05-19) -- `meta.episode_title` left empty despite title regen producing a value. Recent ledger contains: `"episode_title": ""` AND `"title_substitution": {"old_title": "The Geostationary Gambit", "new_title": "Thin Veneer of GEO", "lines_patched": 1, "substitutions": 1}`. Using timestamp fallback 'Signal Lost 20260518 234153'` fires the timestamp fallback. **[FIXED 2026-05-20 -- verified 2026-05-21]** Resolved before this entry was investigated. **Cause:** a Sprint-E "K.5.7" block re-stamped `meta.episode_title` from the raw `episode_title` widget value AFTER the J.5 post-composition title pass; with the widget left blank it overwrote J.5's LLM-regenerated title with `""`, so the video title chain fell to the timestamp last-resort. **Fix:** K.5.7 deleted 2026-05-20; J.5's `meta["episode_title"] = final_title` (`OTR_LedgerScriptWriter.py` L2734) is now the single title authority. **Verify:** `tests/test_writer_stamps_episode_title.py` (3 source-assertion tests pinning the K.5.7 removal) + full `tests/` walk 2441 passed / 0 failed (2026-05-21). **Prior 9 entry:** BUG-LOCAL-235 (2026-05-19) -- HuMo Tier 2 portrait glob naming-contract drift. `_find_portrait` in `nodes/batch_humo_render.py:596-604` globs for `pass1_portrait_*.png` / `otr_humo_pass1_portrait_*.png` (pre-BUG-LOCAL-078 naming). BatchFluxPortraitRender writes `{char_id}_portrait.png` (canonical post-BUG-LOCAL-078 2026-05-04). Tier 2 was never updated to match the new naming. Tier 1 (`cast.portrait_path` stamp) usually catches this -- but when Tier 1 fails (BUG-LOCAL-234 rename-stale fallout being the immediate trigger), Tier 2 also fails because of the glob pattern mismatch, leaving HuMo with no portrait. Two-axis path-resolution failure -- 234 was the ledger-stamp axis, 235 is the glob-pattern axis. Fix landed in the same commit as the BUG-LOCAL-234 HuMo singleton refresh: added Tier 1.5 deterministic-filename fallback at `_find_portrait` -- when cast row name matches speaker but `portrait_path` stamp is missing or stale, also check `portraits_dir / f"{char_id}_portrait.png"` directly. Original Tier 2 (legacy `pass1_portrait_*.png` globs) kept for old episodes; the new Tier 1.5 layer closes the current naming contract. See entry below.
**Prior 10 entry:** BUG-LOCAL-234 (2026-05-18 23:42) -- HuMo / LTX / VideoComposite cannot find portraits + radio bookend after the Video render's per-episode-dir rename. Sequence: writer creates dir `pending_20260518_233621`; Video render renames it to `signal_lost_signal_lost_20260518_234153_20260518_234153`; BatchFluxRender (radio bookend) re-resolves the new path via the BUG-LOCAL-021 singleton fallback and writes to the renamed dir correctly; BUT BatchFluxPortraitRender, BatchHumoRender, BatchLTXRender all retain the pre-rename `episode_id='pending_20260518_233621'` from the in-memory ledger and resolve `portraits_dir`, `radio_still_path`, `output_dir` against the OLD (now-renamed-away) path. HuMo log: `[BatchHumoRender] portraits_dir=C:\Users\jeffr\Documents\ComfyUI\output\otr\episodes\pending_20260518_233621\portraits`. Result: every character line gets `no portrait AND no radio still`; LTX prints `no radio bookend resolved for episode pending_20260518_233621 -- skipping LTX render entirely`; VideoComposite then refuses to proceed: `per_clip_mux: no usable HuMo clips for any line in ledger.lines[]` and the strict-C7 gate rejects fallback. Wall: 525.04 s before VideoComposite raise. NOT caused by BUG-LOCAL-231 fix attempts. Fix shape: replicate the BatchFluxRender singleton-fallback pattern (BUG-LOCAL-021) in HuMo + LTX + VideoComposite -- resolve paths from the LEDGER FILE's actual on-disk location (`Path(ledger_p).parent.parent`) rather than the cached pre-rename `episode_id` string. Pending investigation + fix.
**Prior 11 entry:** BUG-LOCAL-233 (2026-05-18 23:50) -- **VERIFICATION FAILED 2026-05-19 (prompt fix cc553b7 ineffective; new approach needed).** Latest run ledger evidence: 9 `compose_flags.phantom_name` flags including b007 text containing `"Hold tight there, GULLIVER REEVES"` (LEMMY's line addressing GULLIVER by name vocatively) and multiple `"Hold tight, Gulliver"` instances. The strengthened role-induction prompt ("Here, you are now <SPEAKER>. Produce one line/section of dialogue for <SPEAKER>.") **did not eliminate vocative drift** on Mistral-Nemo writer with this seed/style combination. Prompt-level fix alone insufficient; need a **post-compose vocative-strip pass** that removes `OTHER_CAST_NAME` substrings from each line's text before audio render. Reframed scope: was "LineComposer prompt drift", now "vocative drift in composed dialogue requires post-compose mitigation". New entry status: **[FIX LANDED 2026-05-21 -- pending soak verification].** Three-part fix in `nodes/_otr_line_composer.py`: (A2) `_format_last_lines` relabels the announcer's rolling-window entries as `[narration]` instead of `[ANNOUNCER]:` -- this was the real induction surface, since Tier 1 fix #4 (`_derive_prev_speaker`) had already removed "responding to ANNOUNCER" from the prompt back on 2026-05-11; the LLM was echoing the `[ANNOUNCER]:` label it still saw in the LAST SPOKEN block one step above the WRITE LINE slot. (B) a vocative-drift gate in `compose_line` stamps `compose_flags="vocative_drift:ANNOUNCER"` on any non-announcer line addressing the label -- `detect_phantom_names` could never catch it because "ANNOUNCER" is always roster-whitelisted. (C) `strip_announcer_vocative` deterministically removes the comma/boundary-anchored address (trailing / mid / leading shapes) before the line is committed; plain noun references ("the announcer") and real cast-name address ("Alice, run!") are left untouched. cc553b7's prompt strengthening is kept as belt-and-braces; D (reroll) remains the unbuilt documented fallback. LineComposer prompt drift: pre-fix role-induction sentence ("You are <SPEAKER>.") was too weak; the LLM produced dialogue containing literal vocative mentions of OTHER cast members (e.g. line b004's `"It's bigger than any NIST measurement, ANNOUNCER."` in episode pending_20260518_233216, where LEMMY-the-character's line addressed ANNOUNCER by name inside the dialogue text). Strengthened the prompt in commit cc553b7 (`nodes/_otr_line_composer.py:_build_user_prompt` lines 971-984) to `"Here, you are now <SPEAKER>. Produce one line/section of dialogue for <SPEAKER>."` + optional " You are responding to <PREV_SPEAKER>." The "produce... for <SPEAKER>" framing explicitly binds the LLM to speak AS the named speaker, removing the foothold for vocative drift. **NOT a fix for BUG-LOCAL-232 -- this addresses the writer-prompt drift defect only; the underlying cast-generation defect (writer stamping `name='ANNOUNCER'` + `voice_preset='bm_george'` on a character cast row) is fully upstream of the line composer and remains untested + unfixed.** Verification gate for BUG-LOCAL-233: an operator soak run on the real writer LLM produces character lines and zero contain the literal "ANNOUNCER" label (the soak is operator-run -- not reproducible from Cowork). Regression coverage landed with the fix: `tests/test_vocative_drift.py` (17 tests over `strip_announcer_vocative` address shapes, the `_format_last_lines` `[narration]` relabel, and the `compose_line` gate, including the plain-noun and real-cast-name negative cases). Full `tests/` walk 2458 passed / 22 skipped / 0 failed (2026-05-21). **SOAK VERIFICATION FAILED then RE-FIXED 2026-05-21.** Operator writer run, episode signal_lost_singing_asteroid_language_20260521_120244 (commit 6f2abcd): character line b003 reached the ledger as "This isn't some old rock, ANNOUNCER; this thing has a cadence." with empty `compose_flags` -- the strip missed it. b004 confirmed A2/B/C work for the comma/period shapes (drift detected, stripped, `vocative_drift:ANNOUNCER` flagged). Root cause: `_VOCATIVE_MID_RE` (`r",\s*announcer\s*,"`) accepted only a comma as the mid-sentence closing delimiter, so the `, ANNOUNCER;` semicolon shape matched none of the three vocative regexes; since `strip_announcer_vocative` is both detector and stripper, the drift was neither flagged nor stripped (silent miss). Fix: `_VOCATIVE_MID_RE` widened to `r",\s*announcer\s*([,;:])"` with `subn(r"\1 ", ...)` preserving the matched closing delimiter; +3 tests in `test_vocative_drift.py` (20 total -- semicolon + colon strip cases + a compose_line gate test mirroring b003); full `tests/` walk 2461 passed / 22 skipped / 0 failed (2026-05-21). Bug Bible regression not run (survival-guide `tests/` not traversable from Cowork; change is line-composer text logic, no overlap with the Bible's VRAM/ffmpeg/parse-fatal coverage). Status: **[FIX RE-LANDED 2026-05-21 -- pending fresh operator soak]** -- the widened strip is unit-proven; a new writer run is still required to confirm zero "ANNOUNCER" in character text.
**Prior 12 entry:** BUG-LOCAL-232 (2026-05-18 23:34) -- **PENDING INVESTIGATION (NOT FIXED -- prior "fix worked" claim retracted by Jeffrey 2026-05-18 23:50).** Writer cast-lock contract violation. Episode `pending_20260518_233216`: cast.locked produced 2 rows ("announcer + 1 characters, lemmy_hit=True"), but the row at `char_id='c01'` was stamped with `name='ANNOUNCER'` AND `voice_preset='bm_george'` (a Kokoro `bm_*` voice, not a Bark `v2/en_speaker_*`). BatchBarkGenerator.generate_batch caught the misalignment at `nodes/batch_bark_generator.py:439` after 177.01 s: `ValueError: cast.voice_preset missing or non-v2/* for character 'ANNOUNCER' (line_id=b004, char_id='c01', got 'bm_george'). Writer cast-lock contract violation (Gate 1 + Gate 2 should have caught this upstream).` Run never reached FLUX phase. KokoroAnnouncer's BUG-030 line_id stamping is innocent -- this run's chosen voice was `bm_fable`, not `bm_george`, so KokoroAnnouncer cannot have written the cast row. Root cause is in **writer cast generation logic** -- the cast row was malformed at write time (c01 = ANNOUNCER name + Kokoro voice). Cast-lock contract Gates 1+2 should have rejected this shape; they did not. The 2026-05-18 23:30 follow-up run (pending_20260518_233621) did NOT recur this defect, but **that is seed variance, not a fix** -- the cast generator + Gates 1+2 logic have not been touched. **Pending investigation:** read `nodes/_otr_casting.py` Gates 1+2 logic + the cast generator's char_id <-> name <-> voice_preset alignment. Add a regression test that constructs a `(speaker_role='character', char_id_resolving_to_announcer_cast_row)` ledger and asserts Gates 1+2 reject it. See entry below.
**Prior prior prior last entry:** BUG-LOCAL-231 (2026-05-18) -- Residual VRAM pressure + slow FLUX sampler after the BUG-LOCAL-230 fp8 fix. Surfaced by the 2026-05-18 21:10 verification smoke run for BUG-LOCAL-230. With fp8 weights loaded correctly at 13.21 GiB delta=11.08 (gate #4 PASS), the FLUX sampler still ran at 154 s/step (target ~10-15 s) with VRAM peak 15911 MB (over the 14.5 GiB ceiling by ~756 MB) and 1098 MB D3D Shared Memory paging. This is 3.6x faster than the broken pre-fix 564.99 s/it but ~10x slower than the architectural-fix-only target. Separate defect, NOT a dtype-upcast surface. Tagged candidate causes: (a) writer LLM cache stale residency competing for VRAM at FLUX entry; (b) sampler-time launch flag candidates; (c) FLUX CLIP text encoder footprint; (d) FLUX-schnell fallback at 4 steps. **Round-robin gated on candidate ordering -- ChatGPT + Gemini before any code change.** Bible candidate: pending close. See entry below.
**Prior prior prior prior last entry:** BUG-LOCAL-230 (2026-05-18) -- FLUX1-dev-fp8 forced to fp16 by `--force-fp16` ComfyUI launch arg. Closure-run FLUX sampler ran at ~9.4 min/step (564.99 s/it) for 20 steps because flux1-dev-fp8 weights upcast from native fp8 (~11 GiB) to fp16 (~22 GiB) on a 16 GB card, forcing the dynamic offloader to page weights per sampler step. Fix applied across all four launcher sites in the repo + manual launcher: `start_comfy.bat`, `scripts/worker_iter.py` line 549, `scripts/start_comfy_h0_baseline.bat` line 20, `scripts/_start_comfyui.ps1` line 62. **Architectural axis PROVEN by 2026-05-18 21:10 smoke run: gates #1-#4 + #7 PASS** (load delta 11.08 GiB vs predicted ~11 GiB, dtype `torch.float8_e4m3fn` with bf16 cast). Runtime axis FAILING (gates #5 + #6) on a separate defect (BUG-LOCAL-231, residual VRAM pressure). BUG-LOCAL-230 stays at pending-verification posture; promotion to [FIXED] gated on BUG-LOCAL-231 close + clean 7-criteria re-run. Bible candidate: yes -- generalize-able lesson is "global precision flags silently upcast quantized checkpoints (fp8/fp4/NF4/INT8/GGUF) on VRAM-constrained cards; retire the flag in any quantized-checkpoint launcher." See entry below for full details.

## What this file is

Live, append-only record of every bug found in OTR development.
Per CLAUDE.md project rule:

> Maintain `BUG_LOG.md` actively. Every bug logged the moment it's
> found -- no batching, no waiting. Live document tracking the
> build history.

Bugs are numbered `BUG-LOCAL-NNN` with monotonic per-era ranges:

- `001-129` -- pre-voice-path-cleanbreak era (LFC + earlier)
- `200+`    -- voice-path-cleanbreak era (P1-P3, S1-S15)

Numbering reset is intentional -- it gives a clean visual cut
between sprint epochs and lets a reader skim "find me a v2.0-alpha
bug" without scrolling through legacy entries.

---

## Entry schema (per CLAUDE.md)

```markdown
### BUG-LOCAL-NNN: Title
- **Date:** YYYY-MM-DD | **Phase:** 0-6 | **Bible candidate:** yes/no
- **Symptom:** exact error / console output
- **Cause:** root cause (or "pending -- awaiting investigation")
- **Fix:** what resolved it (or "pending")
- **Verify:** how to confirm
- **Tags:** vram, widget-drift, ffmpeg, subprocess, parse-fatal, dialogue-scaling, json-wiring, etc.
```

Mark `[FIXED commit-sha YYYY-MM-DD]` after the title when resolved
-- do not delete entries. When `Bible candidate: yes` and the fix
is verified, promote to the survival guide repo per CLAUDE.md
"Bug Log Pipeline" section.

---

in `docs/known-failures.md`) are NOT bugs in this file's sense -- they

bugs. The `pytest_sessionfinish` hook (S15.1+S15.2 / commit `f813b37`)
enforces that the actual-failure SET stays exactly the quarantine set.

condition; resolution is a product/licensing decision for Jeffrey,
not a mechanical fix). The 6 entries this section previously named
were all promoted 2026-05-13 (s26-downstream sweep); the set was
empty from then until the 2026-05-21 full-walk triage.

Entries below are bugs found in production logic during S6-S15
sprints, regardless of fix-status.

## Voice-path-cleanbreak era (BUG-LOCAL-200+)

### BUG-LOCAL-291: FLUX checkpoint not evicted on portrait-node EXIT -> HuMo Phase C VRAM thrash [FIXED 2026-05-30 -- pending GPU re-run verification]
- **Date:** 2026-05-30 | **Phase:** 5 (FLUX->HuMo boundary) | **Bible candidate:** yes (VRAM-reclaim / executor-cached-patcher class)
- **Symptom:** Live headless run. FLUX cast-portrait render (`OTR_BatchFluxPortraitRender`, FLUX ~11 GB resident) transitioned into HuMo video render (`OTR_BatchHumoRender` Phase C). The inter-phase Lever-1 reclaim logged `allocated 15904 -> 14689 MB, reserved 17280 -> 14720 MB, free=0 MB`, then `PHASE-C-VRAM-PROBE: torch allocated=14689 MB reserved=14720 MB | cuda free=0 MB total=16303 MB | comfy-tracked models=0`. With 0 MB free and 14.69 GB still allocated (OVER the 14.5 GB ceiling), HuMo-1.7B + WanVAE + WanTE + Whisper could not fit -> HuMo sampling thrashed shared memory at ~193 s/it (healthy ~12-14 s/it).
- **Cause:** `OTR_BatchFluxPortraitRender.execute()` pins the FLUX MODEL via `mm.load_models_gpu([model])` on ENTRY (BUG-LOCAL-231 Option B) but had NO eviction on EXIT. Graph path is FLUX-portrait -> `OTR_UnloadAll` -> `OTR_BatchHumoRender`. The downstream UnloadAll (and the in-HuMo `free_otr_pipeline_residue` Lever-1) both run `mm.unload_all_models()`, which clears ComfyUI's model-management REGISTRY (hence `comfy-tracked models=0`) but does NOT drop the strong Python references the ComfyUI executor keeps to the FLUX `OTR_DeferredCheckpointLoader` node's cached `(model, clip, vae)` outputs. Because those ModelPatcher objects stay referenced, `gc.collect()` cannot collect them and `torch.cuda.empty_cache()` cannot reclaim their weight tensors -- so ~14.7 GB of live FLUX weight tensors (`memory_allocated`, not just reserved cache) survived into HuMo Phase C. UnloadAll's own telemetry comment (`visual/unload_all.py`) and `_hard_reset_cuda_context`'s docstring both predicted exactly this "retained reference beyond the model patcher" failure mode.
- **Fix:** Two surgical, complementary changes. (1) `visual/batch_flux_portrait_render.py` -- added an EXIT eviction block at the end of `execute()` (before the final return): move the returned IMAGE batch to CPU; `mm.unload_all_models()`; then explicitly `_evict_patcher()` each of `model`/`clip`/`vae` via `patcher.detach(unpatch_all=True)` (hasattr-guarded, TypeError fallback to no-kwargs `detach()`, final fallback to `patcher.model.to("cpu")`). Detaching the SHARED patcher objects moves their weights off-device even though the executor's cached references stay alive -- which is what `unload_all_models` + `gc` alone cannot do. Then drop local `rendered_imgs` refs, `gc.collect()`, `soft_empty_cache(force=True)`, `synchronize` / `empty_cache` / `ipc_collect`. Logs before/after `memory_allocated`. (2) `nodes/_otr_vram_levers.py::free_otr_pipeline_residue` -- added defense-in-depth step 3a: before `unload_all_models()`, walk `mm.current_loaded_models` and `detach(unpatch_all=True)` each tracked patcher (same fallback ladder), so the in-HuMo Lever-1 and the `OTR_HuMoTierLoader` call site reclaim registry-tracked patchers even in smoke workflows that lack the portrait node. PD1: both edits touch only FLUX MODEL/CLIP/VAE weights (no audio) -- audio path byte-identical. PD2: the FLUX->HuMo transition is NOT an LLM phase boundary, so a full eviction here is correct (no `force_vram_offload` between LLM phases). PD3: no INPUT_TYPES / widget / socket change -> no workflow JSON re-wire needed. PD6: no new LLM call site.
- **Verify:** GPU re-run required (cannot be confirmed headless/CPU). Watch `[OTR_BatchFluxPortraitRender] EXIT eviction (BUG-LOCAL-291): ... allocated X -> Y MB` (expect Y << X, dropping by ~10-14 GB), then the `PHASE-C-VRAM-PROBE` line inside HuMo should show `cuda free >= ~5000 MB` and `torch allocated` well under the 14.5 GB ceiling, and HuMo s/it back to ~12-14 (not ~193). Regression gates (Bug Bible + core + audio-byte-identical) must stay green; the audio assertions are the PD1 guard.
- **Tags:** vram, json-wiring, flux, humo, executor-cached-patcher, model-eviction

### BUG-LOCAL-290: empty `pending_*` episode dirs accumulate when writer aborts at init [FIXED 2026-05-27]
- **Date:** 2026-05-27 | **Phase:** writer entry / housekeeping | **Bible candidate:** yes
- **Symptom:** 17 empty `pending_20260527_*` dirs on disk after one afternoon of soak. Each carried a `*_ledger.json` with 0 lines and 2 meta keys (`episode_id`, `style`) -- the writer's up-front skeleton stamp -- with no follow-on saves. Source: writer node errored at init (RSS fetch failed, LLM load failed, user cancelled queue) BEFORE composing any lines. Successful runs rename their dir to `signal_lost_<title>_<TIMESTAMP>`; aborted runs leave the `pending_*` dir behind forever.
- **Cause:** No cleanup path. The skeleton stamp is intentional (BUG-274 / BUG-278 audit trail) but had no expiry.
- **Fix:** New module `nodes/_otr_pending_cleanup.py` (PURE: stdlib only, no torch / Comfy). `sweep_empty_pending_dirs(episodes_root, *, max_age_seconds=7200, dry_run=False)` walks `output/otr/episodes/` and deletes `pending_*` dirs whose ledger has 0 lines AND whose mtime is older than the threshold (default 2 hours -- well past any clean ~35-min full-pipeline run). PD1: never raises; filesystem errors are caught per-dir and appended to `report.errors`. Wired into `OTR_LedgerScriptWriter.run` immediately AFTER the writer's own skeleton stamp (so the current run self-excludes via the 2-hour age threshold) and BEFORE any expensive work. 12 regression tests in `tests/test_bug_local_290_pending_cleanup.py` pin: deletes old empty / keeps young empty / keeps old-with-lines / deletes old-no-ledger / ignores `signal_lost_*` / dry-run / custom max_age / empty root / nonexistent root / mixed state / report serialization / writer source pin.
- **Verify:** Run writer once; observe `[PendingSweep] BUG-LOCAL-290: deleted N empty pending dir(s)` log line on the next writer queue (the current run's own dir is too young to qualify).
- **Tags:** housekeeping, filesystem, defensive

### BUG-LOCAL-288: SFW post-validator in compose_line [FIXED 2026-05-27]
- **Date:** 2026-05-27 | **Phase:** writer / Stage 3 validators | **Bible candidate:** yes (PD4 hard-rule defense)
- **Symptom:** `pending_20260527_153942` ("Space Force / SpaceX targeting") shipped first character line `"Damn it, we've got thirty-seven minutes before the next signal window closes."` -- mild profanity that PD4 explicitly bans. The whole-episode SFW critic axis catches it post-assembly but compose_line had no per-line gate, so the line landed in the ledger and would have rendered through Bark on a run where Stage 7 didn't escalate.
- **Cause:** Stage 3 validators in `nodes/_otr_stage3_validators.py` did NOT include a profanity gate. `validate_banned_phrases` is the closest sibling but its DEFAULT_BANNED_PHRASES list is purple-prose markers ("ancient secrets", "the void", filler intensifiers), not profanity.
- **Fix:** New validator `validate_sfw(beat, line_text, speaker_name, terms=None)` + new code `CODE_SFW_VIOLATION` + new constant `DEFAULT_PROFANITY_TERMS` (damn / hell / shit / fuck / etc.). Word-boundary regex check (`\b` around each term) so "hell" fires on "go to hell" but NOT on "hello" / "hellish" / "shellfish". Multi-word phrases ("screw you") use substring match. Severity ERROR -- triggers compose_line's single-repair branch (Wave 1 Agent B contract). Wired into `validate_line` BEFORE `validate_banned_phrases` so an SFW-violating line that ALSO contains a banned phrase surfaces the SFW finding first. Reserved speakers (ANNOUNCER / MUSIC) are subject to the same rule (announcer bookends are shippable transcript; PD4 applies uniformly). 15 regression tests in `tests/test_bug_local_288_sfw_validator.py` pin: clean / damn it / hell / hello-passes / hellish-passes / shellfish-passes / case-insensitive / multi-word / punctuation / custom terms override / empty terms / non-string / default list spot check / wiring on bad line / wiring on clean line.
- **Verify:** Live-run a writer that emits a `damn it`-shape line; observe `validation_findings` carries `code=sfw_violation` and one repair regenerate fires.
- **Tags:** validator, SFW, PD4, compose_line, stage3

### BUG-LOCAL-287: Stage 7 critic failure routes to LINE reroll exhaustion [FIXED 2026-05-27]
- **Date:** 2026-05-27 | **Phase:** freeze cascade / Wave 1 Agent C escalation | **Bible candidate:** yes
- **Symptom:** `pending_20260527_170250` (Gemma technical slot, 9-beat superconductivity episode). Stage 7 whole-episode critic emitted empty output twice (`no decodable top-level JSON object found: line 1 column 1 (char 0)`); the shadow stamped a `{'ok': False, 'shadow_setup_failed': True, 'error_type': 'CriticCallFailedError', ...}` marker on `meta['stage7_shadow_critic']` and continued. `decide_escalation_scope` then saw `verdict='' failing_axes=[]` on the marker and fell through to LINE scope (legacy reroll fallback). Legacy reroll loop ran on the Sprint 5B story_critic's separate 6-target list, exhausted its 2-cycle budget, restored pre-reroll lines, stamped `needs_full_rerun`. Bark halted per BUG-LOCAL-276. Episode failed to ship despite multiturn composer landing all 6 character beats with errors=0 or 1.
- **Cause:** Wave 1 Agent C `decide_escalation_scope` had no path for "critic FAILED to produce a verdict". The default fallback (LINE) was correct when there's no Stage 7 opted-in, but wrong when Stage 7 RAN and crashed. Treating both as "no signal" dispatched the legacy reroll on stale Sprint 5B targets that the multiturn composer already produced the best version of.
- **Fix:** New helper `_critic_call_failed(critic_result)` in `nodes/_otr_reroll_escalation.py` distinguishes the four shapes the Stage 7 shadow stamps on failure (CriticCallFailedError marker, shadow_setup_failed marker, skipped marker, dict with error_type/error_message keys) from a real verdict and from the cascade's empty-dict-when-disabled coercion. `decide_escalation_scope` checks the helper AFTER the None fallback and BEFORE the verdict='ship' path. Failed marker -> NONE scope (ship) with `target_beat_ids=[]` and a BUG-LOCAL-287 reason string. Preserves the legacy behaviour: `critic_result is None` still routes to LINE; empty dict (Stage 7 never run) still routes to LINE; real verdicts ('ship' / 'discard') unchanged. 16 regression tests in `tests/test_bug_local_287_critic_failure_ships.py` pin both the helper and the escalation integration.
- **Verify:** Re-queue an episode under the Gemma technical slot where Stage 7 critic returns empty; observe `[LFC] Wave 1 Agent C escalation: scope=none reason=...BUG-LOCAL-287` and the cascade proceeds to freeze_clean instead of needs_full_rerun.
- **Tags:** freeze-cascade, stage7, escalation, gemma, empty-output, wave1c

### BUG-LOCAL-286: doctor edits empty-output -> needs_full_rerun [FIXED 2026-05-27]
- **Date:** 2026-05-27 | **Phase:** freeze cascade / Script Doctor edits pass | **Bible candidate:** yes
- **Symptom:** Recurring across multiple soak runs (`pending_20260527_153942` typical). Diagnosis pass succeeds and flags 2 lines; edits pass attempt 1 returns `""` (`no decodable top-level JSON object found: line 1 column 1 (char 0)`); structural retry at temp=0.3 returns `""`; typed-repair at temp=0.1 returns `""`; ladder exhausted; cascade stamps `needs_full_rerun`. Same root-cause shape as BUG-LOCAL-285 (Mistral-Nemo / Gemma stop_token emit) but on a different call site with a much larger token budget (3500 already), so the BUG-285 max_new_tokens bump doesn't apply.
- **Cause:** `structured_call` retry ladder LOWERS temperature on every retry (0.5 -> 0.3 -> 0.1). That's the correct rule for "malformed JSON" (lower entropy = more in-grammar) but the WRONG rule for "empty output" (lower entropy deepens the stop_token determinism, making the same empty emit MORE likely on the retry, not less).
- **Fix:** Verdict-side, not retry-side. When the exhausted ladder's `last_error` is `JSONDecodeError` containing "line 1 column 1 (char 0)" (the empty-output signature), return `ScriptDoctorReport(edits=[], overall_verdict="clean")` instead of `needs_full_rerun`. Diagnosis pass already named per-line failures in `meta.script_doctor_diagnosis` for the operator's forensic trail; failing the edits pass should not blow up the whole episode (worst case the episode ships with the diagnosis but no rewrites). Other failure shapes (real schema errors, validation errors) STILL route to `needs_full_rerun` -- they signal a doctor LLM that DISAGREES with the diagnosis, a more serious miscalibration. 2 regression tests in `tests/test_bug_local_282_to_285_quad_fix.py::TestBugLocal286DoctorEditsEmptyOutputShipsClean` pin both branches.
- **Verify:** Force a doctor edits call to return `""` (e.g. via the live Gemma technical slot); observe `[OTR_LedgerReviewer:doctor] edits pass exhausted the retry ladder ... with EMPTY output ... returning CLEAN verdict` log line; cascade continues to freeze.
- **Tags:** freeze-cascade, script-doctor, empty-output, structured-call

### BUG-LOCAL-285: run_story_brief_reflection token starvation [FIXED 2026-05-27]
- **Date:** 2026-05-27 | **Phase:** writer / story brief reflection | **Bible candidate:** yes
- **Symptom:** All 3 ladder attempts of `run_story_brief_reflection` returned `""` ("no decodable top-level JSON object found: line 1 column 1 (char 0)"). Not a truncated JSON -- an immediate stop_token before any generation. Cascade stamped `story_brief_status=failed`, MusicGenTheme fell through to v1_atmosphere_vocab, downstream visual / audio pipelines lost the v2 sidecar terms (`music_mood_terms`, `visual_palette`, `key_objects`, `tempo_hint`, `atmosphere_line`).
- **Cause:** `_REFLECTION_MAX_NEW_TOKENS = 160` was set BEFORE the v2 schema added 5 fields (Sprint 8.1 decision A1). The combined v2 JSON output easily wants 200-300 tokens once the 300-char prose `story_brief` + 200-char `atmosphere_line` + five 3-6 term arrays + field-name overhead sum. Mistral-Nemo's constrained decoder hit the cap before it could complete even the first field, returned nothing.
- **Fix:** `_REFLECTION_MAX_NEW_TOKENS` 160 -> 512. Schema-side hard caps still bound the actual prose; the bump just gives the model room to start generating. 3 regression tests in `tests/test_bug_local_282_to_285_quad_fix.py::TestBugLocal285ReflectionTokenBudgetBumped` pin (max_new_tokens >= 512; prompt_version still v2; structural-retry temp still strictly below base = PD invariant).
- **Verify:** Live-run; observe `story_brief_status=ok mood_terms=[...]` instead of `story_brief_status=failed`.
- **Tags:** writer, story-brief, token-starvation, v2-schema

### BUG-LOCAL-284: ReviewerEdit.payload required-str rejected annotate rows [FIXED 2026-05-27]
- **Date:** 2026-05-27 | **Phase:** freeze cascade / Script Doctor edits | **Bible candidate:** yes
- **Symptom:** `run_script_doctor_edits` attempt 1 failed with `1 validation error for ScriptDoctorReport edits.2.payload Field required` whenever the doctor LLM emitted an annotate or skip row without inventing prose payload. The annotate action especially has no natural payload -- the diagnosis pass' `rationale` already carries the doctor's note.
- **Cause:** `class ReviewerEdit` schema had `payload: str` required, no default. Doctor LLM produces `{"line_id": "b002", "action": "annotate", "rationale": "...crucial to the scene"}` (no payload key); pydantic rejects.
- **Fix:** Schema-side relaxation: `payload: str = ""`. Apply step's `annotate` branch updated in lockstep -- empty payload no longer stamps `reviewer_note` (preserves the forensic trail via the diagnosis pass' `rationale` field). The `rewrite` and `skip` branches already had `edit.payload or ""` coercion. 5 regression tests in `tests/test_bug_local_282_to_285_quad_fix.py::TestBugLocal284ReviewerEditPayloadOptional` pin (annotate/skip/rewrite without payload all validate; explicit payload round-trips; source pin on the annotate-branch empty-payload no-op gate).
- **Verify:** Live-run; observe `'run_script_doctor_edits' attempt 1` lands valid instead of failing on `payload Field required`.
- **Tags:** freeze-cascade, script-doctor, schema-relaxation

### BUG-LOCAL-283: build_news_briefs cap of 6 key_terms rejected 7 [FIXED 2026-05-27]
- **Date:** 2026-05-27 | **Phase:** writer / news interpreter | **Bible candidate:** no (small cap bump)
- **Symptom:** `'build_news_briefs' attempt 1 failed: 1 validation error for NewsBriefs key_terms List should have at most 6 items after validation, not 7`. Burned the structural-retry call (attempt 2 at temp=0.35) to land back inside 6. Cost: ~5-10 sec per run, no functional break.
- **Cause:** `_MAX_KEY_TERMS = 6` was the original cap; the LLM consistently wanted to emit 7 distinct terms on news headlines that legitimately have 7 (e.g. NASA / FireSense / Wildfire / Thermal Sensor / Fire Mission / Fire Bulldozer / Firefighter for the Firefighter Safety story).
- **Fix:** `_MAX_KEY_TERMS` 6 -> 7. The Wave 1 Agent A `_judge_term_supported_by_source` semantic check is count-agnostic so downstream behaviour is unchanged. 4 regression tests in `tests/test_bug_local_282_to_285_quad_fix.py::TestBugLocal283NewsBriefsCapBumpedTo7` pin (constant=7; seven validates; eight rejected; zero rejected via schema).
- **Verify:** Live-run; observe `news_interpreter OK: 6 key_terms in 1 attempt(s)` on news headlines that previously took 2 attempts.
- **Tags:** writer, news-interpreter, schema-cap

### BUG-LOCAL-282: Stage1Plan rejected length_target_words=0 on music beats [FIXED 2026-05-27]
- **Date:** 2026-05-27 | **Phase:** writer / Stage 1 shadow plan | **Bible candidate:** yes
- **Symptom:** Every Stage 1 shadow run exhausted its 2-attempt retry budget with `beats.N.length_target_words Input should be greater than or equal to 5 [type=greater_than_equal, input_value=0]`. The LLM was emitting `length_target_words=0` for music_inter beats (`speaker='MUSIC'`) -- a legitimate plan shape since music slots produce no dialogue.
